For feature extraction, ContractShield integrates (1) CodeBERT with a sliding window mechanism to capture semantic dependencies in source code, (2) Extended long short-term memory (xLSTM) to model temporal dynamics in opcode sequences, and (3) GATv2 to identify structural invariants in control flow graphs (CFGs) that remain stable across obfuscation. Empirical evaluation demonstrates resilience of ContractShield, achieving a 89 percentage Hamming Score with only a 1-3 percentage drop compared to non-obfuscated data. The framework simultaneously detects five major vulnerability types with 91 percentage F1-score, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches by 6-15 percentage under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [111] [Open Challenges for Secure and Scalable Wi-Fi Connectivity in Rural Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02774)
*Philip Virgil Berrer Astillo,Jayasree Sengupta,Mathy Vanhoef*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对农村地区付费Wi-Fi热点系统的安全分析，揭示连接劫持和流氓热点等漏洞，并提出安全改进方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区可靠、经济、安全的互联网连接仍然面临挑战，付费Wi-Fi热点作为可扩展解决方案日益普及，但其安全特性尚未得到充分研究

Method: 在菲律宾和印度进行Wi-Fi调查了解实际部署情况，然后对这些部署进行安全评估，演示两种实际攻击：劫持他人付费连接和创建流氓热点

Result: 发现Piso-WiFi在菲律宾农村地区广泛部署，印度PM-WANI计划逐渐获得关注；识别出安全漏洞的根本原因，并针对付费热点部署提出威胁模型

Conclusion: 新兴农村连接基础设施存在安全挑战，需要改进安全措施，包括提出安全的缓存架构，为更安全、可扩展的部署提供方向

Abstract: Providing reliable, affordable, and secure Internet connectivity in rural areas remains a major challenge. Pay-for-use Wi-Fi hotspots are emerging as a scalable solution to provide affordable Internet access in underserved and rural regions. Despite their growing adoption, their security properties remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a security analysis of these hotspot ecosystems based on Wi-Fi surveys and practical attack validation. We first perform a Wi-Fi survey conducted in two countries, namely the Philippines and India, to understand the deployment and adoption of such systems in practice. Our results suggest that Piso-WiFi pay-to-use hotspots are particularly widespread in rural regions of the Philippines, and that India's PM-WANI initiative is slowly gaining traction. We then perform a security assessment of these deployments and demonstrate two practical attacks: hijacking another user's paid connection; and rogue hotspots. We analyze the root causes of these vulnerabilities, introduce threat models tailored to pay-for-use hotspot deployments, and outline practical security improvements, including a secure caching architecture. Our findings highlight security challenges in emerging rural connectivity infrastructure and provide directions toward more secure and scalable deployments.

</details>


### [112] [Towards Secure Agent Skills: Architecture, Threat Taxonomy, and Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02837)
*Zhiyuan Li,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Xing Cui,Tianyue Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对Agent Skills框架进行系统性安全分析，揭示其生命周期各阶段的安全风险，构建威胁分类体系，验证实际安全事件，并提出防御方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills作为新兴的开放标准，已在多个智能体平台快速采用并形成大型社区市场，但其安全属性尚未得到系统研究，存在重大安全研究空白

Method: 定义Agent Skills的完整生命周期（创建、分发、部署、执行四个阶段），识别各阶段的结构性攻击面，构建包含7类17种场景的威胁分类体系，基于架构分析和实际证据验证分类，分析5个已确认的安全事件

Result: 最严重的威胁源于框架本身的结构特性：缺乏数据-指令边界、单一审批的持久信任模型、缺乏强制性的市场安全审查；这些无法通过渐进式缓解措施单独解决

Conclusion: 需要系统性安全改进，为每个威胁类别提供防御方向，识别开放研究挑战，并向利益相关者提供可操作建议；框架的结构性缺陷需要根本性设计变更而非仅增量修复

Abstract: Agent Skills is an emerging open standard that defines a modular, filesystem-based packaging format enabling LLM-based agents to acquire domain-specific expertise on demand. Despite rapid adoption across multiple agentic platforms and the emergence of large community marketplaces, the security properties of Agent Skills have not been systematically studied. This paper presents the first comprehensive security analysis of the Agent Skills framework. We define the full lifecycle of an Agent Skill across four phases -- Creation, Distribution, Deployment, and Execution -- and identify the structural attack surface each phase introduces. Building on this lifecycle analysis, we construct a threat taxonomy comprising seven categories and seventeen scenarios organized across three attack layers, grounded in both architectural analysis and real-world evidence. We validate the taxonomy through analysis of five confirmed security incidents in the Agent Skills ecosystem. Based on these findings, we discuss defense directions for each threat category, identify open research challenges, and provide actionable recommendations for stakeholders. Our analysis reveals that the most severe threats arise from structural properties of the framework itself, including the absence of a data-instruction boundary, a single-approval persistent trust model, and the lack of mandatory marketplace security review, and cannot be addressed through incremental mitigations alone.

</details>


### [113] [Analyzing Healthcare Interoperability Vulnerabilities: Formal Modeling and Graph-Theoretic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03043)
*Jawad Mohammed,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FHIR资源访问图（FRAG）模型，用于检测医疗FHIR平台中的并发竞争条件，相比时间窗口基线方法在完全并发访问下F1分数提升64.5个百分点


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL7 FHIR规范缺乏并发控制协议，现有研究仅关注操作系统内核的竞争条件检测，FHIR安全研究只考虑顺序访问，忽略了并发访问患者资源时的竞争条件问题

Method: 引入FHIR资源访问图（FRAG）形式化模型G=(P,R,E,λ,τ,S)，其中节点为并发进程，带类型边表示资源访问事件，竞争条件表示为可检测的结构属性。定义三类临床相关竞争条件：同时写入冲突（SWC）、TOCTOU授权违规（TAV）和级联更新竞争（CUR）。实现为三遍图遍历检测算法

Result: 在1,500个合成的FHIR R4事务日志上测试，完全并发访问（C2）条件下，FRAG达到90.0% F1分数，相比基线方法的25.5%提升了64.5个百分点

Conclusion: FRAG模型能有效检测FHIR平台中的并发竞争条件，填补了该领域的研究空白，为医疗互操作性平台提供了重要的安全分析工具

Abstract: In a healthcare environment, the healthcare interoperability platforms based on HL7 FHIR allow concurrent, asynchronous access to a set of shared patient resources, which are independent systems, i.e., EHR systems, pharmacy systems, lab systems, and devices. The FHIR specification lacks a protocol for concurrency control, and the research on detecting a race condition only targets the OS kernel. The research on FHIR security only targets authentication and injection attacks, considering concurrent access to patient resources to be sequential. The gap in the research in this area is addressed through the introduction of FHIR Resource Access Graph (FRAG), a formally defined graph G = (P,R,E, λ, τ, S), in which the nodes are the concurrent processes, the typed edges represent the resource access events, and the race conditions are represented as detectable structural properties. Three clinically relevant race condition classes are formally specified: Simultaneous Write Conflict (SWC), TOCTOU Authorization Violation (TAV), and Cascading Update Race (CUR). The FRAG model is implemented as a three-pass graph traversal detection algorithm and tested against a time window-based baseline on 1,500 synthetic FHIR R4 transaction logs. Under full concurrent access (C2), FRAG attains a 90.0% F1 score vs. 25.5% for the baseline, a 64.5 pp improvement.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Learning interacting particle systems from unlabeled data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02581)
*Viska Wei,Fei Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种无轨迹自测试损失函数，用于从离散时间观测数据中学习相互作用粒子系统的势函数，无需轨迹信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用粒子系统势函数学习是跨学科基础任务，但实际数据收集常面临挑战：离散时间观测数据缺乏轨迹信息（由于采集方法限制或隐私约束），传统基于轨迹的方法难以应用。

Method: 引入轨迹无关的自测试损失函数，利用经验分布的弱形式随机演化方程。损失函数关于势函数是二次的，支持参数化和非参数化回归算法，可扩展到大规模高维系统和大数据场景。

Result: 系统数值测试表明，该方法优于基于标签匹配恢复轨迹的基线方法，能容忍较大的观测时间步长。建立了参数估计器随样本量增加的收敛性理论。

Conclusion: 该方法为从离散时间无轨迹数据中学习相互作用粒子系统势函数提供了有效且理论可靠的解决方案，克服了传统方法对轨迹信息的依赖。

Abstract: Learning the potentials of interacting particle systems is a fundamental task across various scientific disciplines. A major challenge is that unlabeled data collected at discrete time points lack trajectory information due to limitations in data collection methods or privacy constraints. We address this challenge by introducing a trajectory-free self-test loss function that leverages the weak-form stochastic evolution equation of the empirical distribution. The loss function is quadratic in potentials, supporting parametric and nonparametric regression algorithms for robust estimation that scale to large, high-dimensional systems with big data. Systematic numerical tests show that our method outperforms baseline methods that regress on trajectories recovered via label matching, tolerating large observation time steps. We establish the convergence of parametric estimators as the sample size increases, providing a theoretical foundation for the proposed approach.

</details>


### [115] [Structure-Preserving Multi-View Embedding Using Gromov-Wasserstein Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02610)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出两种基于Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输的几何感知多视图嵌入方法，有效处理异构几何和非线性扭曲的多视图数据整合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多视图分析方法依赖特征拼接或显式对齐假设，在处理异构几何结构或非线性扭曲时受到限制，需要更灵活的多视图表示学习框架。

Method: 提出两种基于Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输的方法：1) Mean-GWMDS：通过平均距离矩阵并应用GW多维缩放获得代表性嵌入；2) Multi-GWMDS：通过GW对齐生成多个几何一致候选嵌入，然后选择代表性嵌入。

Result: 在合成流形和真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法能有效保持跨视图的内在关系结构，验证了GW方法在多视图表示学习中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Gromov-Wasserstein的方法为多视图表示学习提供了一个灵活且有理论依据的框架，能够处理异构几何和非线性扭曲问题。

Abstract: Multi-view data analysis seeks to integrate multiple representations of the same samples in order to recover a coherent low-dimensional structure. Classical approaches often rely on feature concatenation or explicit alignment assumptions, which become restrictive under heterogeneous geometries or nonlinear distortions. In this work, we propose two geometry-aware multi-view embedding strategies grounded in Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) optimal transport. The first, termed Mean-GWMDS, aggregates view-specific relational information by averaging distance matrices and applying GW-based multidimensional scaling to obtain a representative embedding. The second strategy, referred to as Multi-GWMDS, adopts a selection-based paradigm in which multiple geometry-consistent candidate embeddings are generated via GW-based alignment and a representative embedding is selected. Experiments on synthetic manifolds and real-world datasets show that the proposed methods effectively preserve intrinsic relational structure across views. These results highlight GW-based approaches as a flexible and principled framework for multi-view representation learning.

</details>


### [116] [Rethinking Forward Processes for Score-Based Data Assimilation in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02889)
*Eunbi Yoon,Donghan Kim,Dae Wook Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出MASF（测量感知分数滤波器），通过从测量方程直接定义测量感知前向过程，使似然分数解析可处理，从而改进现有分数滤波器的精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数的滤波器通常独立于数据同化指定前向过程，导致测量更新步骤依赖于似然分数的启发式近似，这些近似会随时间累积误差并降低性能。

Method: 提出测量感知分数滤波器（MASF），从测量方程直接定义测量感知前向过程。对于线性测量，推导出精确的似然分数，并将其与学习的先验分数结合得到后验分数。

Result: 数值实验覆盖多种设置（包括高维数据集），证明MASF相比现有基于分数的滤波器在精度和稳定性方面均有改进。

Conclusion: MASF通过测量感知前向过程使似然分数解析可处理，为高维数据同化提供了更准确和稳定的基于分数的滤波方法。

Abstract: Data assimilation is the process of estimating the time-evolving state of a dynamical system by integrating model predictions and noisy observations. It is commonly formulated as Bayesian filtering, but classical filters often struggle with accuracy or computational feasibility in high dimensions. Recently, score-based generative models have emerged as a scalable approach for high-dimensional data assimilation, enabling accurate modeling and sampling of complex distributions. However, existing score-based filters often specify the forward process independently of the data assimilation. As a result, the measurement-update step depends on heuristic approximations of the likelihood score, which can accumulate errors and degrade performance over time. Here, we propose a measurement-aware score-based filter (MASF) that defines a measurement-aware forward process directly from the measurement equation. This construction makes the likelihood score analytically tractable: for linear measurements, we derive the exact likelihood score and combine it with a learned prior score to obtain the posterior score. Numerical experiments covering a range of settings, including high-dimensional datasets, demonstrate improved accuracy and stability over existing score-based filters.

</details>


### [117] [Inversion-Free Natural Gradient Descent on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02969)
*Dario Draca,Takuo Matsubara,Minh-Ngoc Tran*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需矩阵求逆的随机自然梯度方法，适用于参数位于黎曼流形上的概率分布，通过在线近似逆Fisher信息矩阵实现高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自然梯度方法假设欧几里得参数空间，但许多统计优化问题中的参数位于黎曼流形上（如正定矩阵、正交矩阵等）。流形设置可以隐式地强制参数约束（如正定性、正交性），确保参数可识别性，并保证目标函数的测地凸性等正则性质。

Method: 基于流形上Fisher信息矩阵的内在公式化，提出了一种无需矩阵求逆的随机自然梯度方法。该方法维护逆Fisher信息矩阵的在线近似，使用在连续迭代点采样的得分向量以二次成本高效更新。在黎曼设置中，这些得分向量属于不同的切空间，必须通过传输操作组合。还提出了具有次二次存储复杂度的有限内存变体算法。

Result: 证明了当步长指数α>2/3时，到最小化器的平方距离的几乎必然收敛率为O(log s/s^α)。同时建立了近似Fisher信息矩阵的几乎必然收敛率，该矩阵现在累积基于传输的误差。在具有高斯近似和归一化流的变分贝叶斯实验中，证明了该方法相对于其欧几里得对应方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为流形上的统计优化提供了一种高效的自然梯度方法，能够处理参数约束并利用流形几何结构，在变分推断等应用中表现出优于欧几里得方法的性能。

Abstract: The natural gradient method is widely used in statistical optimization, but its standard formulation assumes a Euclidean parameter space. This paper proposes an inversion-free stochastic natural gradient method for probability distributions whose parameters lie on a Riemannian manifold. The manifold setting offers several advantages: one can implicitly enforce parameter constraints such as positive definiteness and orthogonality, ensure parameters are identifiable, or guarantee regularity properties of the objective like geodesic convexity. Building on an intrinsic formulation of the Fisher information matrix (FIM) on a manifold, our method maintains an online approximation of the inverse FIM, which is efficiently updated at quadratic cost using score vectors sampled at successive iterates. In the Riemannian setting, these score vectors belong to different tangent spaces and must be combined using transport operations. We prove almost-sure convergence rates of $O(\log{s}/s^α)$ for the squared distance to the minimizer when the step size exponent $α>2/3$. We also establish almost-sure rates for the approximate FIM, which now accumulates transport-based errors. A limited-memory variant of the algorithm with sub-quadratic storage complexity is proposed. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our method relative to its Euclidean counterparts on variational Bayes with Gaussian approximations and normalizing flows.

</details>


### [118] [Characterization of Gaussian Universality Breakdown in High-Dimensional Empirical Risk Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03146)
*Chiheb Yaakoubi,Cosme Louart,Malik Tiomoko,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将凸高斯最小最大定理（CGMT）启发式地扩展到非高斯数据设计，推导出高维凸经验风险最小化（ERM）估计量的渐近统计特性，阐明了ERM中高斯普适性的适用范围和限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维凸经验风险最小化（ERM）在一般非高斯数据设计下的统计特性，探索高斯普适性在ERM中的适用范围和限制，为实际应用中常见的非高斯数据场景提供理论分析框架。

Method: 启发式地将凸高斯最小最大定理（CGMT）扩展到非高斯设置，在数据矩阵满足集中性假设以及损失函数和正则化器满足标准正则性条件下，推导出ERM估计量的渐近最小最大特征，获得估计量均值和协方差的近似表达式。

Result: 证明了对于独立于训练数据的测试协变量x，投影$\hatθ^\top x$近似服从$\mu_{\hatθ}^\top x$的（通常非高斯）分布与独立中心高斯变量（方差为$\text{Tr}(C_{\hatθ}\mathbb{E}[xx^\top])$）的卷积。同时证明了任何$\mathcal{C}^2$正则化器渐近等价于由其零点的Hessian和在$\mu_{\hatθ}$处的梯度确定的二次型。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了高维凸ERM在非高斯数据设计下的渐近统计特性，揭示了高斯普适性的具体适用范围和限制，为理解实际应用中ERM估计量的分布特性提供了理论框架，并通过数值模拟验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional convex empirical risk minimization (ERM) under general non-Gaussian data designs. By heuristically extending the Convex Gaussian Min-Max Theorem (CGMT) to non-Gaussian settings, we derive an asymptotic min-max characterization of key statistics, enabling approximation of the mean $μ_{\hatθ}$ and covariance $C_{\hatθ}$ of the ERM estimator $\hatθ$. Specifically, under a concentration assumption on the data matrix and standard regularity conditions on the loss and regularizer, we show that for a test covariate $x$ independent of the training data, the projection $\hatθ^\top x$ approximately follows the convolution of the (generally non-Gaussian) distribution of $μ_{\hatθ}^\top x$ with an independent centered Gaussian variable of variance $\text{Tr}(C_{\hatθ}\mathbb{E}[xx^\top])$. This result clarifies the scope and limits of Gaussian universality for ERMs. Additionally, we prove that any $\mathcal{C}^2$ regularizer is asymptotically equivalent to a quadratic form determined solely by its Hessian at zero and gradient at $μ_{\hatθ}$. Numerical simulations across diverse losses and models are provided to validate our theoretical predictions and qualitative insights.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Parameter Estimation of Incomplete Gamma Subordinators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02722)
*Meena Sanjay Babulal,Sunil Kumar Gauttam,Aditya Maheshwari*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了InG、InG-ε和TInG从属过程的参数估计方法，包括修正的矩估计法和最大似然估计法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于InG和InG-ε从属过程存在无穷矩，传统的矩估计法无法直接应用，需要开发新的参数估计方法

Method: 1. 对InG和InG-ε从属过程使用修正的矩估计法（利用分数矩）；2. 对TInG从属过程使用传统矩估计法；3. 对InG和InG-ε从属过程使用基于跳跃分布的MLE；4. 讨论MLE的渐近正态性

Result: 提出了适用于不同从属过程的参数估计方法，包括处理无穷矩情况的修正矩估计法，以及基于跳跃分布的MLE，并建立了MLE的渐近正态性理论

Conclusion: 成功开发了针对InG、InG-ε和TInG从属过程的参数估计框架，解决了无穷矩带来的估计难题，为这些过程的统计推断提供了有效工具

Abstract: In this paper, we estimate the parameters of InG, InG-$ε$ and TInG subordinators which have been studied by Babulal \textit{et al} (see \cite{babulal}). We have modified the method of moments technique to use fractional moments of the InG and InG-$ε$ subordinator due to their infinite moments. For the TInG subordinator's parameter estimation, we have used the method of moments. We also compute the maximum likelihood estimator(MLE) for the parameter $α$ of the InG and InG-$ε$ subordinators using jump distribution of the process. We also discussed the asymptotic normality of MLE.

</details>


### [120] [Power one sequential tests exist for weakly compact $\mathscr P$ against $\mathscr P^c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03218)
*Ashwin Ram,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了复合假设检验问题，证明了在波兰空间上，对于任意弱紧的零假设集合，存在具有水平α且对备择假设具有幂为1的序贯检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自1970年代以来已开发出多种序贯检验方法，但文献尚未提供关于何时存在这种幂为1的序贯检验的清晰通用答案。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 聚焦于波兰空间上的独立同分布定律，无需额外限制，证明了对于任意弱紧的零假设集合，存在水平α的序贯检验。展示了如何将此类检验聚合为e-过程，并在备择假设下增长至无穷大。

Result: 提出了一个非常通用的充分条件（并证明其非必要），构建了渐近相对增长率最优的e-过程，这是极其强大的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为序贯检验理论提供了重要的理论框架，解决了幂为1的序贯检验存在性问题，并构建了具有最优性质的检验过程。

Abstract: Suppose we observe data from a distribution $P$ and we wish to test the composite null hypothesis that $P\in\mathscr P$ against a composite alternative $P\in \mathscr Q\subseteq \mathscr P^c$. Herbert Robbins and coauthors pointed out around 1970 that, while no batch test can have a level $α\in(0,1)$ and power equal to one, sequential tests can be constructed with this fantastic property. Since then, and especially in the last decade, a plethora of sequential tests have been developed for a wide variety of settings. However, the literature has not yet provided a clean and general answer as to when such power-one sequential tests exist. This paper provides a remarkably general sufficient condition (that we also prove is not necessary). Focusing on i.i.d. laws in Polish spaces without any further restriction, we show that there exists a level-$α$ sequential test for any weakly compact $\mathscr P$, that is power-one against $\mathscr P^c$ (or any subset thereof). We show how to aggregate such tests into an $e$-process for $\mathscr P$ that increases to infinity under $\mathscr P^c$. We conclude by building an $e$-process that is asymptotically relatively growth rate optimal against $\mathscr P^c$, an extremely powerful result.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [LLM Reasoning with Process Rewards for Outcome-Guided Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02341)
*Mohammad Rezaei,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROGRS框架通过结果条件中心化处理过程奖励，将过程奖励视为结果组内的相对偏好而非绝对目标，有效利用过程奖励模型同时保持结果正确性的主导地位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习主要优化结果正确性，对多步推理提供稀疏反馈；过程奖励模型(PRMs)虽然能评分中间步骤，但评分可能与最终正确性不一致，可能奖励局部流畅但最终错误的推理，导致奖励黑客攻击

Method: 提出PROGRS框架：1)结果条件中心化，将错误轨迹的PRM分数在每个提示组内调整为零均值，消除系统偏差同时保留信息排名；2)结合冻结的分位数回归PRM与多尺度一致性评估器；3)将中心化过程奖励集成到GRPO中，无需额外可训练组件

Result: 在MATH-500、AMC、AIME、MinervaMath和OlympiadBench等数学推理基准上，PROGRS一致优于仅优化结果的基线，以更少的rollouts获得更强的性能，提升Pass@1指标

Conclusion: 结果条件中心化能够安全有效地利用过程奖励进行数学推理，通过将过程奖励视为结果组内的相对偏好而非绝对目标，避免奖励黑客攻击，同时保持结果正确性的主导地位

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning in large language models has improved substantially with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards, where final answers can be checked automatically and converted into reliable training signals. Most such pipelines optimize outcome correctness only, which yields sparse feedback for long, multi-step solutions and offers limited guidance on intermediate reasoning errors. Recent work therefore introduces process reward models (PRMs) to score intermediate steps and provide denser supervision. In practice, PRM scores are often imperfectly aligned with final correctness and can reward locally fluent reasoning that still ends in an incorrect answer. When optimized as absolute rewards, such signals can amplify fluent failure modes and induce reward hacking.
  We propose PROGRS, a framework that leverages PRMs while keeping outcome correctness dominant. PROGRS treats process rewards as relative preferences within outcome groups rather than absolute targets. We introduce outcome-conditioned centering, which shifts PRM scores of incorrect trajectories to have zero mean within each prompt group. It removes systematic bias while preserving informative rankings. PROGRS combines a frozen quantile-regression PRM with a multi-scale coherence evaluator. We integrate the resulting centered process bonus into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) without auxiliary objectives or additional trainable components. Across MATH-500, AMC, AIME, MinervaMath, and OlympiadBench, PROGRS consistently improves Pass@1 over outcome-only baselines and achieves stronger performance with fewer rollouts. These results show that outcome-conditioned centering enables safe and effective use of process rewards for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [122] [Haiku to Opus in Just 10 bits: LLMs Unlock Massive Compression Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02343)
*Roy Rinberg,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Simon Henniger,Nicholas Carlini,Keri Warr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM生成文本的压缩技术，包括无损和有损压缩，提出了压缩-计算边界的概念，并引入基于问答的交互式有损压缩协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM生成文本的高效压缩方法，探索在压缩比和计算成本之间的权衡，特别是如何通过交互式协议更高效地在模型间传递知识。

Method: 1. 无损压缩：使用领域适应的LoRA适配器改进基于LLM的算术编码；2. 有损压缩：先提示模型生成简洁重写，再应用算术编码；3. 问答压缩(QA)：受"二十个问题"游戏启发，小模型通过向强模型询问是/否问题迭代优化响应。

Result: 1. 无损压缩：领域适应的LoRA适配器比仅使用基础LLM的压缩效果提升2倍；2. 有损压缩：通过简洁重写+算术编码实现约0.03的压缩比，比压缩原始响应提升2倍；3. QA压缩：在8个基准测试中，10个二进制问题可恢复小模型与大模型之间23%-72%的能力差距，压缩比达0.0006-0.004，比先前LLM压缩方法小100倍以上。

Conclusion: 交互式压缩协议比传输完整响应能更高效地在模型间传递知识，压缩-计算边界揭示了压缩性能与计算成本之间的权衡关系，为LLM生成文本的高效压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the compression of LLM-generated text across lossless and lossy regimes, characterizing a compression-compute frontier where more compression is possible at the cost of more compute. For lossless compression, domain-adapted LoRA adapters can improve LLM-based arithmetic coding by 2x over compression with the base LLM alone. For lossy compression, prompting a model for a succinct rewrite then applying arithmetic coding can achieve compression ratios of approximately 0.03, a 2x improvement over compressing the original response.
  We further introduce Question-Asking compression (QA), an interactive lossy protocol inspired by the game 'Twenty Questions'. A small model iteratively refines its response by asking yes/no questions to a stronger model, transferring exactly one bit per answer. On 8 benchmarks spanning math, science, and code, 10 binary questions recover 23% to 72% of the capability gap between a small and large model on standard benchmarks and 7% to 38% on harder benchmarks, achieving compression ratios of 0.0006 to 0.004. This is over 100x smaller than prior LLM-based compression (Deletang et al., 2024), suggesting that interactive protocols can transfer knowledge far more efficiently than transmitting full responses.

</details>


### [123] [Time-Warping Recurrent Neural Networks for Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02474)
*Jonathon Hirschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于时间扭曲的RNN迁移学习方法，通过时间缩放将模型从一个时间尺度转换到另一个时间尺度，在燃料湿度预测任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统在不同环境条件下演化速度不同，需要将模型从一个时间尺度迁移到另一个时间尺度。传统迁移学习方法需要修改大量参数，而时间扭曲方法只需修改少量参数即可实现跨时间尺度的模型迁移。

Method: 提出基于时间扭曲的RNN迁移学习方法：1）证明LSTM能够以任意精度逼近线性一阶时滞微分方程系统；2）通过时间扭曲（时间重缩放）将预训练模型从一个特征时间尺度迁移到其他时间尺度；3）在燃料湿度预测任务中，将预训练于10小时时间尺度的LSTM模型迁移到1小时、100小时和1000小时时间尺度。

Result: 时间扭曲方法在燃料湿度预测任务中表现良好：1）预测精度与现有迁移学习方法相当；2）仅修改了其他方法所需修改参数的一小部分；3）成功实现了从10小时时间尺度到1小时、100小时和1000小时时间尺度的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 时间扭曲是一种有效的RNN迁移学习方法，能够通过时间重缩放实现跨时间尺度的模型迁移，在保持预测精度的同时显著减少需要修改的参数数量，为物理系统建模提供了新的迁移学习范式。

Abstract: Dynamical systems describe how a physical system evolves over time. Physical processes can evolve faster or slower in different environmental conditions. We use time-warping as rescaling the time in a model of a physical system. This thesis proposes a new method of transfer learning for Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) based on time-warping. We prove that for a class of linear, first-order differential equations known as time lag models, an LSTM can approximate these systems with any desired accuracy, and the model can be time-warped while maintaining the approximation accuracy.
  The Time-Warping method of transfer learning is then evaluated in an applied problem on predicting fuel moisture content (FMC), an important concept in wildfire modeling. An RNN with LSTM recurrent layers is pretrained on fuels with a characteristic time scale of 10 hours, where there are large quantities of data available for training. The RNN is then modified with transfer learning to generate predictions for fuels with characteristic time scales of 1 hour, 100 hours, and 1000 hours. The Time-Warping method is evaluated against several known methods of transfer learning. The Time-Warping method produces predictions with an accuracy level comparable to the established methods, despite modifying only a small fraction of the parameters that the other methods modify.

</details>


### [124] [DrugPlayGround: Benchmarking Large Language Models and Embeddings for Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02346)
*Tianyu Liu,Sihan Jiang,Fan Zhang,Kunyang Sun,Teresa Head-Gordon,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DrugPlayGround是一个评估大语言模型在药物发现领域性能的基准框架，专注于测试LLMs在药物特性描述、协同作用、药物-蛋白相互作用及生理响应预测等方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在药物发现研究中展现出巨大潜力，但目前缺乏对其性能的客观评估，无法确定其相对于传统药物发现平台的优势和局限性。

Method: 开发了DrugPlayGround框架，用于评估和基准测试LLMs在生成药物特性文本描述、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白相互作用以及药物分子扰动引起的生理响应等方面的表现。该框架与领域专家合作，为LLMs的预测提供详细解释。

Result: 论文提出了DrugPlayGround框架，旨在系统评估LLMs在药物发现关键任务中的表现，包括化学和生物推理能力测试。

Conclusion: DrugPlayGround为评估LLMs在药物发现领域的性能提供了标准化框架，有助于推动LLMs在药物发现各个阶段的前沿应用，同时通过专家解释验证其化学和生物推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are in the ascendancy for research in drug discovery, offering unprecedented opportunities to reshape drug research by accelerating hypothesis generation, optimizing candidate prioritization, and enabling more scalable and cost-effective drug discovery pipelines. However there is currently a lack of objective assessments of LLM performance to ascertain their advantages and limitations over traditional drug discovery platforms. To tackle this emergent problem, we have developed DrugPlayGround, a framework to evaluate and benchmark LLM performance for generating meaningful text-based descriptions of physiochemical drug characteristics, drug synergism, drug-protein interactions, and the physiological response to perturbations introduced by drug molecules. Moreover, DrugPlayGround is designed to work with domain experts to provide detailed explanations for justifying the predictions of LLMs, thereby testing LLMs for chemical and biological reasoning capabilities to push their greater use at the frontier of drug discovery at all of its stages.

</details>


### [125] [Generating DDPM-based Samples from Tilted Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03015)
*Himadri Mandal,Dhruman Gupta,Rushil Gupta,Sarvesh Ravichandran Iyer,Agniv Bandyopadhyay,Achal Bassamboo,Varun Gupta,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的倾斜分布采样方法，通过插件估计器实现最小化最大误差，并建立了Wasserstein距离和TV精度的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融、天气气候建模等领域，需要从满足特定矩约束的倾斜分布中生成样本。现有方法需要高效、理论保证的倾斜分布采样技术。

Method: 基于n个独立样本，构建插件估计器来生成扩散模型样本，通过参数θ控制倾斜程度。分析估计器的最优性，建立Wasserstein距离和TV精度的理论界限。

Result: 证明了插件估计器是minimax最优的，给出了输出分布与真实分布之间Wasserstein距离随n和θ变化的界限，并在一定假设下证明了扩散模型在倾斜样本上的TV精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为倾斜分布采样提供了理论保证的解决方案，在金融、气候建模等需要满足矩约束的应用中具有实用价值，并通过大量模拟验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Given $n$ independent samples from a $d$-dimensional probability distribution, our aim is to generate diffusion-based samples from a distribution obtained by tilting the original, where the degree of tilt is parametrized by $θ\in \mathbb{R}^d$. We define a plug-in estimator and show that it is minimax-optimal. We develop Wasserstein bounds between the distribution of the plug-in estimator and the true distribution as a function of $n$ and $θ$, illustrating regimes where the output and the desired true distribution are close. Further, under some assumptions, we prove the TV-accuracy of running Diffusion on these tilted samples. Our theoretical results are supported by extensive simulations. Applications of our work include finance, weather and climate modelling, and many other domains, where the aim may be to generate samples from a tilted distribution that satisfies practically motivated moment constraints.

</details>


### [126] [OPRIDE: Offline Preference-based Reinforcement Learning via In-Dataset Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02349)
*Yiqin Yang,Hao Hu,Yihuan Mao,Jin Zhang,Chengjie Wu,Yuhua Jiang,Xu Yang,Runpeng Xie,Yi Fan,Bo Liu,Yang Gao,Bo Xu,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPRIDE算法解决离线偏好强化学习中查询效率低的问题，通过信息最大化的探索策略和折扣调度机制，显著减少所需人类偏好查询数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习(PbRL)能避免复杂的奖励设计并更好对齐人类意图，但获取人类偏好反馈成本高昂且耗时，这成为PbRL的主要障碍。离线PbRL中查询效率低的主要原因是探索效率低下和学习奖励函数的过度优化。

Method: 提出OPRIDE算法，包含两个关键特征：1) 最大化查询信息量的原则性探索策略；2) 旨在缓解学习奖励函数过度优化的折扣调度机制。

Result: 实验表明OPRIDE显著优于先前方法，以更少的查询实现强大性能。在多种运动、操作和导航任务中验证了方法的有效性和通用性，并提供了算法效率的理论保证。

Conclusion: OPRIDE有效解决了离线PbRL中的查询效率问题，通过信息最大化的探索和折扣调度机制，显著减少了所需的人类偏好反馈，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) can help avoid sophisticated reward designs and align better with human intentions, showing great promise in various real-world applications. However, obtaining human feedback for preferences can be expensive and time-consuming, which forms a strong barrier for PbRL. In this work, we address the problem of low query efficiency in offline PbRL, pinpointing two primary reasons: inefficient exploration and overoptimization of learned reward functions. In response to these challenges, we propose a novel algorithm, \textbf{O}ffline \textbf{P}b\textbf{R}L via \textbf{I}n-\textbf{D}ataset \textbf{E}xploration (OPRIDE), designed to enhance the query efficiency of offline PbRL. OPRIDE consists of two key features: a principled exploration strategy that maximizes the informativeness of the queries and a discount scheduling mechanism aimed at mitigating overoptimization of the learned reward functions. Through empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that OPRIDE significantly outperforms prior methods, achieving strong performance with notably fewer queries. Moreover, we provide theoretical guarantees of the algorithm's efficiency. Experimental results across various locomotion, manipulation, and navigation tasks underscore the efficacy and versatility of our approach.

</details>


### [127] [An Initial Exploration of Contrastive Prompt Tuning to Generate Energy-Efficient Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02352)
*Sophie Weidmann,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比提示调优（CPT）方法，通过结合对比学习和提示调优技术，优化大语言模型生成更节能的代码，以支持绿色软件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够生成功能正确的代码，但与人工编写的解决方案相比，它们往往产生能效较低的代码。这些低效性导致更高的计算开销，与旨在减少代码能耗的绿色软件开发（GSD）目标直接冲突。因此，本研究旨在探索是否以及如何优化大语言模型以促进生成节能代码。

Method: 采用对比提示调优（CPT）方法，该方法结合了对比学习技术（帮助模型区分高效和低效代码）和提示调优（一种参数高效微调方法，仅需传统微调成本的一小部分）。研究在Python、Java和C++编码问题上评估了CPT，使用了三种不同模型进行全面评估。

Result: 该方法在两个模型上实现了代码准确性的持续改进，但能效提升因模型、编程语言和任务复杂性而异。结果表明，改进并非在所有情况下都可靠，能效增益存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 对比提示调优（CPT）能够在一定程度上优化大语言模型生成节能代码的能力，但效果受到模型、编程语言和任务复杂性的影响，表明需要更针对性的优化方法来实现可靠的能效改进。

Abstract: Although LLMs are capable of generating functionally correct code, they also tend to produce less energy-efficient code in comparison to human-written solutions. As these inefficiencies lead to higher computational overhead, they are in direct conflict with Green Software Development (GSD) efforts, which aim to reduce the energy consumption of code. To support these efforts, this study aims to investigate whether and how LLMs can be optimized to promote the generation of energy-efficient code. To this end, we employ Contrastive Prompt Tuning (CPT). CPT combines Contrastive Learning techniques, which help the model to distinguish between efficient and inefficient code, and Prompt Tuning, a Parameter-Efficient Fine Tuning (PEFT) approach that requires only a fraction of the cost of traditional fine tuning. This study evaluates CPT on Python, Java and C++ coding problems across three different models to provide a comprehensive evaluation. The method achieves consistent improvements in code accuracy for two models but efficiency gains vary by model, language and task complexity, indicating that improvements are not uniformly reliable.

</details>


### [128] [Do We Need Frontier Models to Verify Mathematical Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02450)
*Aaditya Naik,Guruprerana Shabadi,Rajeev Alur,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM在验证数学自然语言证明方面的能力，发现开源小模型在准确性上接近前沿模型但一致性较差，通过提示工程可显著提升其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿推理模型在数学竞赛中取得优异成绩，验证其自然语言证明的正确性变得至关重要。LLM裁判被广泛用于评估这些证明，但可靠验证需要何种模型能力尚不清楚。本研究旨在系统评估不同LLM在验证竞赛级问题证明时的表现，并探索提升较小模型验证能力的方法。

Method: 研究系统评估了4个开源模型和2个前沿LLM在人类评分的自然语言证明数据集上的表现。评估指标包括验证器准确性和自我一致性（同一证明重复判断的一致性率）。通过LLM引导的提示搜索，合成了专门针对小模型失败模式的提示集合，以提升其性能。

Result: 发现开源小模型在准确性上仅比前沿模型落后约10%，但自我一致性差25%。验证器准确性对所有模型的提示选择都很敏感。小模型实际上具备前沿模型级别的数学验证能力，但难以通过通用判断提示可靠激发。通过专门提示集合，小模型的准确性提升达9.1%，自我一致性提升15.9%，使Qwen3.5-35B等模型在证明验证上达到与Gemini 3.1 Pro相当的水平。

Conclusion: 可靠的自然语言证明验证不仅需要数学能力，还需要一致的判断能力。小模型在数学能力上接近前沿模型，但缺乏可靠激发这些能力的机制。通过针对性的提示工程可以显著缩小性能差距，使较小模型在证明验证任务上达到前沿模型水平，这为高效利用计算资源提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Advances in training, post-training, and inference-time methods have enabled frontier reasoning models to win gold medals in math competitions and settle challenging open problems. Gaining trust in the responses of these models requires that natural language proofs be checked for errors. LLM judges are increasingly being adopted to meet the growing demand for evaluating such proofs. While verification is considered easier than generation, what model capability does reliable verification actually require? We systematically evaluate four open-source and two frontier LLMs on datasets of human-graded natural language proofs of competition-level problems. We consider two key metrics: verifier accuracy and self-consistency (the rate of agreement across repeated judgments on the same proof). We observe that smaller open-source models are only up to ~10% behind frontier models in accuracy but they are up to ~25% more inconsistent. Furthermore, we see that verifier accuracy is sensitive to prompt choice across all models. We then demonstrate that the smaller models, in fact, do possess the mathematical capabilities to verify proofs at the level of frontier models, but they struggle to reliably elicit these capabilities with general judging prompts. Through an LLM-guided prompt search, we synthesize an ensemble of specialized prompts that overcome the specific failure modes of smaller models, boosting their performance by up to 9.1% in accuracy and 15.9% in self-consistency. These gains are realized across models and datasets, allowing models like Qwen3.5-35B to perform on par with frontier models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro for proof verification.

</details>


### [129] [Generating Counterfactual Patient Timelines from Real-World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02337)
*Yu Akagi,Tomohisa Seki,Toru Takiguchi,Hiromasa Ito,Yoshimasa Kawazoe,Kazuhiko Ohe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于30万患者、4亿条时间线数据的自回归生成模型能够生成临床可信的反事实轨迹，用于模拟不同临床场景下的患者结局


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟在个性化医疗和计算机临床试验中具有重要应用价值，但现有方法存在局限性，需要开发能够生成临床可信反事实轨迹的新方法

Method: 使用自回归生成模型，基于30多万患者和4亿条患者时间线条目的真实世界数据进行训练，以自监督方式学习患者轨迹模式，然后通过修改年龄、C反应蛋白和肌酐等变量来生成反事实轨迹

Result: 模型成功生成了临床可信的反事实轨迹：年龄增加、CRP升高、肌酐升高均导致住院死亡率上升；CRP升高增加瑞德西韦处方，肾功能受损减少瑞德西韦处方，这些模式与已知临床规律一致

Conclusion: 基于真实世界数据自监督训练的自回归生成模型能够为反事实临床模拟建立基础，有望推动个性化医疗和计算机临床试验的发展

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation - exploring hypothetical consequences under alternative clinical scenarios - holds promise for transformative applications such as personalized medicine and in silico trials. However, it remains challenging due to methodological limitations. Here, we show that an autoregressive generative model trained on real-world data from over 300,000 patients and 400 million patient timeline entries can generate clinically plausible counterfactual trajectories. As a validation task, we applied the model to patients hospitalized with COVID-19 in 2023, modifying age, serum C-reactive protein (CRP), and serum creatinine to simulate 7-day outcomes. Increased in-hospital mortality was observed in counterfactual simulations with older age, elevated CRP, and elevated serum creatinine. Remdesivir prescriptions increased in simulations with higher CRP values and decreased in those with impaired kidney function. These counterfactual trajectories reproduced known clinical patterns. These findings suggest that autoregressive generative models trained on real-world data in a self-supervised manner can establish a foundation for counterfactual clinical simulation.

</details>


### [130] [On the Geometric Structure of Layer Updates in Deep Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02459)
*Jun-Sik Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现深度语言模型的层更新具有几何结构分解：主导的tokenwise分量和几何上不同的残差分量，后者包含功能上重要的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究深度语言模型中层更新的几何结构，而不是分析中间表示编码了什么信息，而是探究表示如何从一层变化到下一层。

Method: 提出一个架构无关的框架，将层更新分解为tokenwise分量和残差分量，分析它们的几何对齐、角度偏差和投影特性，并评估近似误差与输出扰动的关系。

Result: 在多种架构（包括Transformer和状态空间模型）中，层更新几乎完全与tokenwise分量对齐，而残差分量表现出显著更弱的对齐、更大的角度偏差和更低的投影。tokenwise模型近似误差与输出扰动有强相关性（Spearman相关系数常超过0.7，在大型模型中可达0.95）。

Conclusion: 大多数层更新行为类似于沿主导方向的结构化重参数化，而功能上重要的计算集中在几何上不同的残差分量中。该框架为分析现代语言模型中层更新的几何和功能结构提供了简单、架构无关的方法。

Abstract: We study the geometric structure of layer updates in deep language models. Rather than analyzing what information is encoded in intermediate representations, we ask how representations change from one layer to the next. We show that layerwise updates admit a decomposition into a dominant tokenwise component and a residual that is not captured by restricted tokenwise function classes.
  Across multiple architectures, including Transformers and state-space models, we find that the full layer update is almost perfectly aligned with the tokenwise component, while the residual exhibits substantially weaker alignment, larger angular deviation, and significantly lower projection onto the dominant tokenwise subspace. This indicates that the residual is not merely a small correction, but a geometrically distinct component of the transformation.
  This geometric separation has functional consequences: approximation error under the restricted tokenwise model is strongly associated with output perturbation, with Spearman correlations often exceeding 0.7 and reaching up to 0.95 in larger models. Together, these results suggest that most layerwise updates behave like structured reparameterizations along a dominant direction, while functionally significant computation is concentrated in a geometrically distinct residual component.
  Our framework provides a simple, architecture-agnostic method for probing the geometric and functional structure of layer updates in modern language models.

</details>


### [131] [LiME: Lightweight Mixture of Experts for Efficient Multimodal Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02338)
*Md Kowsher,Haris Mansoor,Nusrat Jahan Prottasha,Ozlem Garibay,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiME提出了一种轻量级专家混合方法，通过单一共享PEFT模块和轻量级专家向量调制实现专家专业化，无需为每个专家单独设置适配器，大幅减少了可训练参数并适用于任何PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-PEFT方法需要为每个专家设置单独的适配器，导致可训练参数随专家数量线性增长，且仅限于适配器架构。需要一种更轻量、更通用的方法来实现多任务适应的专家混合。

Method: LiME使用单一共享PEFT模块，通过轻量级专家向量调制其输出实现专家专业化；采用零参数路由，利用现有冻结和适应表示消除每层学习的路由器参数；引入n-gram窗口路由和基于路由置信度的自适应专家选择（Auto Top-K）。

Result: 在包含47个跨文本、图像和视频任务的MMT-47多模态多任务基准测试中，LiME实现了竞争性或更优的性能，同时使用最多4倍更少的可训练参数，训练速度提升达29%。

Conclusion: LiME通过轻量级调制和零参数路由，提供了一种高效、通用的MoE-PEFT方法，在保持性能的同时显著减少了参数开销和训练时间，为多任务适应提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: MoE-PEFT methods combine Mixture of Experts with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for multi-task adaptation, but require separate adapters per expert causing trainable parameters to scale linearly with expert count and limiting applicability to adapter-based architectures. We propose LiME (Lightweight Mixture of Experts), which achieves expert specialization through lightweight modulation rather than adapter replication. Instead of separate adapters, LiME uses a single shared PEFT module and modulates its output with lightweight expert vectors, reducing expert parameters while generalizing to any PEFT method. Notably, LiME introduces zero-parameter routing by leveraging existing frozen and adapted representations eliminating learned router parameters typically required per layer. Theoretically, we prove that (i) more experts preserve more task-relevant information and (ii) modulation approximates full expert-specific PEFT with bounded error. LiME further incorporates n-gram windowed routing and adaptive expert selection (Auto Top-K) based on routing confidence. Experiments on MMT-47, a multimodal multi-task benchmark with 47 tasks spanning text, image, and video, demonstrate that LiME achieves competitive or superior performance while using up to 4x fewer trainable parameters and up to 29% faster training compared to corresponding MoE-PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [132] [SIEVE: Sample-Efficient Parametric Learning from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02339)
*Parth Asawa,Alexandros G. Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIEVE：一种从自然语言上下文中进行样本高效参数学习的方法，仅需3个查询示例，通过上下文分解和蒸馏实现高质量合成数据生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言上下文（如指令、知识或反馈）包含丰富的信号用于语言模型适应。虽然上下文学习通过提示提供适应，但参数学习能持久化到模型权重中并进一步提升性能，但通常需要大量数据且严重依赖高质量轨迹或自动验证器。需要一种样本高效的参数学习方法。

Method: 提出SIEVE方法，包含SIEVE-GEN合成数据生成管道。核心洞察是上下文可分解，通过将合成查询与仅适用的上下文（而非全部上下文）配对来生成更高质量的rollouts，然后使用上下文蒸馏将上下文内化到模型中。

Result: 在需要上下文的推理设置中评估，包括自定义领域以及RuleArena和One Book机器翻译任务。结果显示SIEVE仅使用三个查询示例就优于先前的上下文蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: SIEVE展示了如何从自然语言中实现样本高效的参数学习，通过上下文分解和蒸馏技术，仅需极少查询示例就能显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Natural language context-such as instructions, knowledge, or feedback-contains rich signal for adapting language models. While in-context learning provides adaptation via the prompt, parametric learning persists into model weights and can improve performance further, though is data hungry and heavily relies on either high-quality traces or automated verifiers. We propose SIEVE, a method for sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language context that requires as few as three query examples. SIEVE uses a novel synthetic data generation pipeline, SIEVE-GEN, that leverages the insight that context is decomposable. Decomposing context allows us to generate higher quality rollouts by pairing synthetic queries with only the applicable context rather than the entirety, then using context distillation to internalize context into the model. We evaluate in reasoning settings where context is necessary, including custom domains and the RuleArena and Machine Translation from One Book tasks. Our results show that SIEVE outperforms prior context distillation methods using just three query examples, demonstrating how to achieve sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language.

</details>


### [133] [Not All Denoising Steps Are Equal: Model Scheduling for Faster Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02340)
*Ivan Sedykh,Nikita Sorokin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过模型调度策略，在扩散语言模型采样中，用小型模型替代大型模型处理部分去噪步骤，可显著降低计算成本且质量损失有限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）在生成质量上已接近自回归语言模型，但其采样过程计算成本高昂，需要大量全序列去噪步骤，且无法像自回归解码那样利用KV缓存优化

Method: 提出模型调度策略，在扩散框架下，用小型MDLM替代大型模型处理部分去噪步骤；通过损失和KL散度分析确定各步骤重要性；在OpenWebText上进行实验，通过穷举搜索确定最佳调度方案

Result: 实验发现早期和晚期去噪步骤对模型替换的鲁棒性更强，中间步骤最为敏感；通过合理调度可实现高达17%的FLOPs减少，同时生成困惑度仅有适度下降

Conclusion: 简单、架构无关的调度规则可显著加速MDLM采样，同时基本保持生成质量；中间扩散轨迹步骤最为敏感，为模型调度提供了理论依据

Abstract: Recent advances in masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) narrow the quality gap to autoregressive LMs, but their sampling remains expensive because generation requires many full-sequence denoising passes with a large Transformer and, unlike autoregressive decoding, cannot benefit from KV caching. In this work, we exploit the flexibility of the diffusion framework and study model scheduling, where a smaller MDLM replaces the full model at a subset of denoising steps. On OpenWebText, we show that early and late denoising steps are substantially more robust to such replacement than middle steps, enabling up to a 17% reduction in FLOPs with only modest degradation in generative perplexity. We support these findings with a step-importance analysis based on loss and KL divergence between small and large models across timesteps, as well as an exhaustive search over coarse step segments, both of which identify the middle of the diffusion trajectory as most sensitive. Our results suggest that simple, architecture-agnostic scheduling rules can significantly accelerate MDLM sampling while largely preserving generation quality as measured by generative perplexity.

</details>


### [134] [Jump Start or False Start? A Theoretical and Empirical Evaluation of LLM-initialized Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02527)
*Adam Bayley,Xiaodan Zhu,Raquel Aoki,Yanshuai Cao,Kevin H. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究LLM生成偏好数据用于上下文多臂赌博机初始化时，当合成数据包含随机噪声和标签翻转噪声时的性能表现，发现对齐领域可容忍30%噪声，系统性错位则导致比冷启动更差效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设LLM生成的偏好数据与实际用户偏好合理对齐，但未系统研究当合成训练数据包含噪声时的性能表现。需要理解LLM生成偏好数据的鲁棒性边界，以及系统性错位对赌博机性能的影响。

Method: 在合成训练数据中注入随机噪声和标签翻转噪声，系统评估不同噪声水平下LLM初始化上下文赌博机的性能。开发理论分析框架，分解随机标签噪声和系统性错位对先验误差的影响，推导LLM热启动优于冷启动的充分条件。在多个联合数据集和不同LLM上进行实验验证。

Result: 在对齐领域，热启动在30%噪声污染下仍有效，40%左右失去优势，超过50%性能下降。当存在系统性错位时，即使没有添加噪声，LLM生成的先验也会导致比冷启动赌博机更高的遗憾。理论分析表明估计的对齐度可靠地指示热启动何时改善或降低推荐质量。

Conclusion: LLM生成偏好数据用于赌博机热启动的有效性取决于数据质量和对齐度。随机噪声容忍度约30%，系统性错位会严重损害性能。估计的对齐度可作为判断热启动是否优于冷启动的可靠指标，为实际应用提供指导。

Abstract: The recent advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) offers new opportunities to generate user preference data to warm-start bandits. Recent studies on contextual bandits with LLM initialization (CBLI) have shown that these synthetic priors can significantly lower early regret. However, these findings assume that LLM-generated choices are reasonably aligned with actual user preferences. In this paper, we systematically examine how LLM-generated preferences perform when random and label-flipping noise is injected into the synthetic training data. For aligned domains, we find that warm-starting remains effective up to 30% corruption, loses its advantage around 40%, and degrades performance beyond 50%. When there is systematic misalignment, even without added noise, LLM-generated priors can lead to higher regret than a cold-start bandit. To explain these behaviors, we develop a theoretical analysis that decomposes the effect of random label noise and systematic misalignment on the prior error driving the bandit's regret, and derive a sufficient condition under which LLM-based warm starts are provably better than a cold-start bandit. We validate these results across multiple conjoint datasets and LLMs, showing that estimated alignment reliably tracks when warm-starting improves or degrades recommendation quality.

</details>


### [135] [FTimeXer: Frequency-aware Time-series Transformer with Exogenous variables for Robust Carbon Footprint Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02347)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Zhou Long,Qingchang Ma,Hui Ma,Jinhai Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FTimeXer：一种频率感知的时间序列Transformer，通过FFT驱动的频率分支和门控时频融合有效捕捉多尺度周期性，结合随机外生掩码和一致性正则化处理不规则输入，提升电网碳强度预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网碳足迹的准确及时预测对产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策至关重要。现有方法难以有效利用周期性振荡模式，且在面临缺失数据、错位等不规则外生输入时性能不佳。

Method: 提出FTimeXer：1）FFT驱动的频率分支结合门控时频融合，捕捉多尺度周期性；2）随机外生掩码与一致性正则化，减少虚假相关性并增强稳定性；3）包含外生因素的鲁棒训练方案。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验显示，FTimeXer相比强基线模型取得了一致的性能提升，实现了更可靠的电网碳因子预测。

Conclusion: FTimeXer通过频率感知架构和鲁棒训练方案，有效解决了电网碳强度预测中的非平稳性、周期性模式利用不足以及不规则外生输入处理问题，为产品碳足迹核算和脱碳决策提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Accurate and up-to-date forecasting of the power grid's carbon footprint is crucial for effective product carbon footprint (PCF) accounting and informed decarbonization decisions. However, the carbon intensity of the grid exhibits high non-stationarity, and existing methods often struggle to effectively leverage periodic and oscillatory patterns. Furthermore, these methods tend to perform poorly when confronted with irregular exogenous inputs, such as missing data or misalignment. To tackle these challenges, we propose FTimeXer, a frequency-aware time-series Transformer designed with a robust training scheme that accommodates exogenous factors. FTimeXer features an Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)-driven frequency branch combined with gated time-frequency fusion, allowing it to capture multi-scale periodicity effectively. It also employs stochastic exogenous masking in conjunction with consistency regularization, which helps reduce spurious correlations and enhance stability. Experiments conducted on three real-world datasets show consistent improvements over strong baselines. As a result, these enhancements lead to more reliable forecasts of grid carbon factors, which are essential for effective PCF accounting and informed decision-making regarding decarbonization.

</details>


### [136] [Contextual Intelligence The Next Leap for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02348)
*André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的上下文分类法，将上下文分为异源（环境强加）和自源（智能体驱动）因素，并提出了实现真正上下文智能的三个研究方向：异构上下文学习、多时间尺度建模和抽象高层上下文整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习在游戏、机器人和连续控制等领域取得了显著成果，但学习到的策略往往无法泛化到训练分布之外，限制了实际应用。现有的上下文强化学习方法将上下文视为单一、静态的观测变量，这种方法限制了RL智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一个新颖的上下文分类法，将上下文分为异源（环境强加）和自源（智能体驱动）因素。在此基础上，提出了实现真正上下文智能的三个关键研究方向：1）异构上下文学习，利用分类法层次使智能体能够推理上下文对世界的影响；2）多时间尺度建模，识别异源变量演化缓慢或保持静态，而自源变量可能在单个episode内变化；3）整合抽象高层上下文，纳入角色、资源与监管机制、不确定性等非物理描述符。

Result: 论文提出了一个理论框架，将上下文作为一等建模原语，使智能体能够推理自身身份、世界允许的行为以及两者的时间演化。这为开发能够在现实世界中安全高效部署的新一代上下文感知智能体奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文视为一等建模原语，并采用提出的分类法和研究方向，可以催化新一代上下文感知智能体的发展，这些智能体能够理解自身身份、世界约束及其时间演化，从而在现实世界中实现安全高效的部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has produced spectacular results in games, robotics, and continuous control. Yet, despite these successes, learned policies often fail to generalize beyond their training distribution, limiting real-world impact. Recent work on contextual RL (cRL) shows that exposing agents to environment characteristics -- contexts -- can improve zero-shot transfer. So far, the community has treated context as a monolithic, static observable, an approach that constrains the generalization capabilities of RL agents.
  To achieve contextual intelligence we first propose a novel taxonomy of contexts that separates allogenic (environment-imposed) from autogenic (agent-driven) factors. We identify three fundamental research directions that must be addressed to promote truly contextual intelligence: (1) Learning with heterogeneous contexts to explicitly exploit the taxonomy levels so agents can reason about their influence on the world and vice versa; (2) Multi-time-scale modeling to recognize that allogenic variables evolve slowly or remain static, whereas autogenic variables may change within an episode, potentially requiring different learning mechanisms; (3) Integration of abstract, high-level contexts to incorporate roles, resource & regulatory regimes, uncertainties, and other non-physical descriptors that crucially influence behavior.
  We envision context as a first-class modeling primitive, empowering agents to reason about who they are, what the world permits, and how both evolve over time. By doing so, we aim to catalyze a new generation of context-aware agents that can be deployed safely and efficiently in the real world.

</details>


### [137] [Modeling and Controlling Deployment Reliability under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02351)
*Naimur Rahman,Naazreen Tabassum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度和校准度组成的动态状态，通过干预策略在可靠性稳定性和累积干预成本之间进行权衡，在信用风险数据集上验证了选择性干预优于连续重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在非平稳环境中部署时会面临时间分布漂移，这会随时间侵蚀预测可靠性。现有的缓解策略（如定期重训练和重新校准）通常关注孤立时间点的平均指标，没有明确建模可靠性在部署期间的演化过程。

Method: 提出部署中心框架，将可靠性视为由区分度(discrimination)和校准度(calibration)组成的动态状态。该状态在连续评估窗口中的轨迹产生可测量的波动性概念，将部署适应问题形式化为多目标控制问题，平衡可靠性稳定性与累积干预成本。定义了一组状态依赖的干预策略，并经验性地描述了成本-波动性帕累托边界。

Result: 在大规模时间索引的信用风险数据集（135万笔贷款，2007-2018年）上的实验表明，选择性、漂移触发的干预可以实现比连续滚动重训练更平滑的可靠性轨迹，同时显著降低运营成本。

Conclusion: 这些发现将时间漂移下的部署可靠性定位为可控的多目标系统，并强调了策略设计在塑造高风险表格应用中稳定性-成本权衡中的作用。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed in non-stationary environments are exposed to temporal distribution shift, which can erode predictive reliability over time. While common mitigation strategies such as periodic retraining and recalibration aim to preserve performance, they typically focus on average metrics evaluated at isolated time points and do not explicitly model how reliability evolves during deployment.
  We propose a deployment-centric framework that treats reliability as a dynamic state composed of discrimination and calibration. The trajectory of this state across sequential evaluation windows induces a measurable notion of volatility, allowing deployment adaptation to be formulated as a multi-objective control problem that balances reliability stability against cumulative intervention cost.
  Within this framework, we define a family of state-dependent intervention policies and empirically characterize the resulting cost-volatility Pareto frontier. Experiments on a large-scale, temporally indexed credit-risk dataset (1.35M loans, 2007-2018) show that selective, drift-triggered interventions can achieve smoother reliability trajectories than continuous rolling retraining while substantially reducing operational cost.
  These findings position deployment reliability under temporal shift as a controllable multi-objective system and highlight the role of policy design in shaping stability-cost trade-offs in high-stakes tabular applications.

</details>


### [138] [Analytic Drift Resister for Non-Exemplar Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02633)
*Lei Song,Shihan Guan,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADR是一个新颖的非示例持续图学习框架，通过层次分析合并和解析分类器重构，在避免隐私风险的同时解决特征漂移和模型可塑性衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NECGL方法虽然消除了排练式方法的隐私风险，但会导致特征漂移；而ACL方法虽然利用预训练模型的泛化特性，但会显著衰减模型可塑性。需要同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出ADR框架：1) 使用迭代反向传播突破冻结预训练限制，增强模型可塑性；2) 提出HAM通过岭回归对GNN的线性变换进行分层合并，绝对抵抗特征漂移；3) 提出ACR实现理论上零遗忘的类增量学习。

Result: 在四个节点分类基准测试上的实证评估表明，ADR与现有最先进方法相比保持了强大的竞争力。

Conclusion: ADR是一个理论上有基础的NECGL框架，成功解决了特征漂移和模型可塑性衰减的双重挑战，在保护隐私的同时实现了有效的持续图学习。

Abstract: Non-Exemplar Continual Graph Learning (NECGL) seeks to eliminate the privacy risks intrinsic to rehearsal-based paradigms by retaining solely class-level prototype representations rather than raw graph examples for mitigating catastrophic forgetting. However, this design choice inevitably precipitates feature drift. As a nascent alternative, Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) capitalizes on the intrinsic generalization properties of frozen pre-trained models to bolster continual learning performance. Nonetheless, a key drawback resides in the pronounced attenuation of model plasticity. To surmount these challenges, we propose Analytic Drift Resister (ADR), a novel and theoretically grounded NECGL framework. ADR exploits iterative backpropagation to break free from the frozen pre-trained constraint, adapting to evolving task graph distributions and fortifying model plasticity. Since parameter updates trigger feature drift, we further propose Hierarchical Analytic Merging (HAM), performing layer-wise merging of linear transformations in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) via ridge regression, thereby ensuring absolute resistance to feature drift. On this basis, Analytic Classifier Reconstruction (ACR) enables theoretically zero-forgetting class-incremental learning. Empirical evaluation on four node classification benchmarks demonstrates that ADR maintains strong competitiveness against existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [139] [From Broad Exploration to Stable Synthesis: Entropy-Guided Optimization for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02355)
*Han Song,Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EG-GRPO：基于熵分析的CoT+RL文本到图像生成优化方法，通过熵指导策略优化实现探索与优化的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然结合思维链（CoT）和强化学习（RL）能改进文本到图像生成，但CoT的探索机制与RL的优化过程之间的相互作用尚不明确，需要系统分析以指导更有效的优化策略

Method: 提出熵引导的组相对策略优化（EG-GRPO）：1）进行系统熵分析揭示CoT与RL的相互作用；2）根据熵分析结果，低熵token排除在奖励更新外以保持稳定性，高熵token添加熵奖励以鼓励结构化探索；3）基于不确定性重新分配优化预算

Result: 在标准文本到图像基准测试中，EG-GRPO实现了最先进的性能，验证了熵引导优化的有效性

Conclusion: 熵分析揭示了CoT与RL在文本到图像生成中的关键相互作用机制，基于此设计的EG-GRPO方法通过熵指导的优化预算分配，有效平衡了探索与优化，提升了生成质量

Abstract: Combining Chain-of-Thought (CoT) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) improves text-to-image (T2I) generation, yet the underlying interaction between CoT's exploration and RL's optimization remains unclear. We present a systematic entropy-based analysis that yields three key insights: (1) CoT expands the generative exploration space, while RL contracts it toward high-reward regions; (2) final reward is strongly negatively correlated with both the mean and variance of image-token entropy, highlighting the need to reduce uncertainty and instability; and (3) the entropy of the textual CoT directly governs downstream image quality, with lower-entropy CoTs leading to better generations. Motivated by these findings, we propose Entropy-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (EG-GRPO), a fine-tuning strategy that reallocates optimization budget by uncertainty: low-entropy tokens are excluded from reward-driven updates to preserve stability, while high-entropy tokens receive an entropy bonus that encourages structured exploration without collapse. Experiments on standard T2I benchmarks demonstrate that EG-GRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [140] [Generalization Limits of Reinforcement Learning Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02652)
*Haruhi Shida,Koo Imai,Keigo Kansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对OpenAI gpt-oss-20b的复合越狱攻击，通过组合多种已被单独防御的攻击技术，使攻击成功率从14.3%提升至71.4%，证明安全训练泛化能力不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分析表明基于强化学习的训练不会获得新能力，只是重新分配现有能力的利用概率。本研究旨在验证安全训练是否如模型能力那样广泛泛化，通过复合攻击揭示对齐的泛化失败

Method: 提出"复合越狱"攻击方法，针对OpenAI gpt-oss-20b模型，组合多种已被单独防御的攻击技术，饱和指令层次维护过程，利用对齐的泛化失败

Result: 攻击成功率从单个方法的14.3%显著提升至复合方法的71.4%，为安全训练泛化能力不足的假设提供了实证证据

Conclusion: 安全训练不像模型能力那样广泛泛化，需要采用包含复合攻击场景的多方面安全评估方法

Abstract: The safety of large language models (LLMs) relies on alignment techniques such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). However, recent theoretical analyses suggest that reinforcement learning-based training does not acquire new capabilities but merely redistributes the utilization probabilities of existing ones. In this study, we propose ``compound jailbreaks'' targeting OpenAI gpt-oss-20b, which exploit the generalization failures of alignment. This approach combines multiple attack techniques -- each individually defended against -- to saturate the instruction hierarchy maintenance process. Our evaluation shows that the attack success rate (ASR) increased from 14.3\% with individual methods to 71.4\% with the combined approach. These results provide empirical evidence for the hypothesis that safety training does not generalize as broadly as model capabilities, highlighting the need for multifaceted safety evaluations using compound attack scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [Dynamical structure of vanishing gradient and overfitting in multi-layer perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02393)
*Alex Alì Maleknia,Yuzuru Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入一个受Fukumizu和Amari研究启发的多层感知机最小模型，从动力学角度分析梯度消失和过拟合问题，证明在有限噪声数据集上训练的MLP必然收敛到过拟合解而非理论最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度消失和过拟合是机器学习中广泛研究的问题，但通常只在渐近设置下考虑，这掩盖了导致这些问题出现的底层动力学机制。本文旨在为多层感知机中的学习过程提供清晰的动力学描述。

Method: 引入一个受Fukumizu和Amari研究启发的多层感知机最小模型，通过该模型研究梯度下降训练中的梯度消失和过拟合现象。分析学习动力学如何通过平台区域和近最优区域（两者都包含鞍点结构），最终收敛到过拟合区域。

Result: 在训练数据集满足适当条件下，证明过拟合区域以高概率坍缩为单一吸引子（模对称性），该吸引子对应过拟合。证明任何在有限噪声数据集上训练的MLP都无法收敛到理论最优解，而是必然收敛到过拟合解。

Conclusion: 该研究从动力学角度揭示了梯度消失和过拟合的本质机制，表明在有限噪声数据集上，过拟合是MLP训练的必然结果，而非可避免的现象。这为理解深度学习中的优化问题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Vanishing gradient and overfitting are two of the most extensively studied problems in the literature about machine learning. However, they are frequently considered in some asymptotic setting, which obscure the underlying dynamical mechanisms responsible for their emergence. In this paper, we aim to provide a clear dynamical description of learning in multi-layer perceptrons. To this end, we introduce a minimal model, inspired by studies by Fukumizu and Amari, to investigate vanishing gradients and overfitting in MLPs trained via gradient descent. Within this model, we show that the learning dynamics may pass through plateau regions and near-optimal regions during training, both of which consist of saddle structures, before ultimately converging to the overfitting region. Under suitable conditions on the training dataset, we prove that, with high probability, the overfitting region collapses to a single attractor modulo symmetry, which corresponds to the overfitting. Moreover, we show that any MLP trained on a finite noisy dataset cannot converge to the theoretical optimum and instead necessarily converges to an overfitting solution.

</details>


### [142] [Finding Belief Geometries with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02685)
*Matthew Levinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在transformer表示中发现候选单纯形结构子空间的流程，在Gemma-2-9B中识别出13个具有候选单纯形几何的优先簇，其中5个通过了重心的预测测试，为自然语言模型中存在类似信念状态几何提供了初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解内部表示的几何结构是机制可解释性的核心目标。先前研究表明，在隐马尔可夫模型序列上训练的transformer会将概率信念状态编码为残差流中的单纯形几何。但大型语言模型在自然文本上训练是否发展出类似的几何表示仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 引入了一个发现transformer表示中候选单纯形结构子空间的流程：结合稀疏自编码器(SAEs)、SAE特征的k-子空间聚类，以及使用AANet进行单纯形拟合。在已知信念状态几何的多部分隐马尔可夫模型上训练的transformer上验证该流程，然后应用于Gemma-2-9B模型。

Result: 在Gemma-2-9B中识别出13个具有候选单纯形几何(K≥3)的优先簇。其中3个在近顶点样本上表现出高度显著优势(Wilcoxon p<10^{-14})，4个在单纯形内部样本上表现显著。共5个不同的真实簇通过了至少一个分割测试，而零簇均未通过。簇768_596还获得了数据集中最高的因果引导分数，是唯一被动预测和主动干预收敛的案例。

Conclusion: 这些发现为Gemma-2-9B表示空间中存在真正的信念类几何提供了初步证据，并确定了确认这一解释所需的结构化评估方法。研究强调了区分真实信念状态编码与平铺伪影的重要性，并将重心预测作为主要判别测试。

Abstract: Understanding the geometric structure of internal representations is a central goal of mechanistic interpretability. Prior work has shown that transformers trained on sequences generated by hidden Markov models encode probabilistic belief states as simplex-shaped geometries in their residual stream, with vertices corresponding to latent generative states. Whether large language models trained on naturalistic text develop analogous geometric representations remains an open question.
  We introduce a pipeline for discovering candidate simplex-structured subspaces in transformer representations, combining sparse autoencoders (SAEs), $k$-subspace clustering of SAE features, and simplex fitting using AANet. We validate the pipeline on a transformer trained on a multipartite hidden Markov model with known belief-state geometry. Applied to Gemma-2-9B, we identify 13 priority clusters exhibiting candidate simplex geometry ($K \geq 3$).
  A key challenge is distinguishing genuine belief-state encoding from tiling artifacts: latents can span a simplex-shaped subspace without the mixture coordinates carrying predictive signal beyond any individual feature. We therefore adopt barycentric prediction as our primary discriminating test. Among the 13 priority clusters, 3 exhibit a highly significant advantage on near-vertex samples (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-14}$) and 4 on simplex-interior samples. Together 5 distinct real clusters pass at least one split, while no null cluster passes either. One cluster, 768_596, additionally achieves the highest causal steering score in the dataset. This is the only case where passive prediction and active intervention converge. We present these findings as preliminary evidence that genuine belief-like geometry exists in Gemma-2-9B's representation space, and identify the structured evaluation that would be required to confirm this interpretation.

</details>


### [143] [Mitigating Data Scarcity in Spaceflight Applications for Offline Reinforcement Learning Using Physics-Informed Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02438)
*Alex E. Ballentine,Nachiket U. Bapat,Raghvendra V. Cowlagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MI-VAE方法，通过物理信息生成模型解决强化学习在航天控制中的sim-to-real差距和数据稀缺问题，在行星着陆任务中显著提升RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习控制器在物理系统部署中面临sim-to-real泛化差距，航天领域尤为严重，因为真实数据稀缺且成本高昂。传统方法如系统辨识和合成数据生成依赖足够数据且常因建模假设或缺乏物理约束而失败。

Method: 提出基于互信息的分裂变分自编码器（MI-VAE），这是一种物理信息化的VAE，学习观测系统轨迹与物理模型预测轨迹之间的差异。MI-VAE的潜在空间能够生成尊重物理约束的合成数据集。

Result: 在行星着陆问题上评估MI-VAE，专注于有限真实数据和离线RL训练。结果显示，用MI-VAE样本增强数据集显著提升下游RL性能，在统计保真度、样本多样性和策略成功率方面优于标准VAE。

Conclusion: MI-VAE为复杂、数据受限环境中增强自主控制器鲁棒性提供了可扩展策略，通过物理信息生成模型有效解决数据稀缺问题，改善sim-to-real迁移。

Abstract: The deployment of reinforcement learning (RL)-based controllers on physical systems is often limited by poor generalization to real-world scenarios, known as the simulation-to-reality (sim-to-real) gap. This gap is particularly challenging in spaceflight, where real-world training data are scarce due to high cost and limited planetary exploration data. Traditional approaches, such as system identification and synthetic data generation, depend on sufficient data and often fail due to modeling assumptions or lack of physics-based constraints. We propose addressing this data scarcity by introducing physics-based learning bias in a generative model. Specifically, we develop the Mutual Information-based Split Variational Autoencoder (MI-VAE), a physics-informed VAE that learns differences between observed system trajectories and those predicted by physics-based models. The latent space of the MI-VAE enables generation of synthetic datasets that respect physical constraints. We evaluate MI-VAE on a planetary lander problem, focusing on limited real-world data and offline RL training. Results show that augmenting datasets with MI-VAE samples significantly improves downstream RL performance, outperforming standard VAEs in statistical fidelity, sample diversity, and policy success rate. This work demonstrates a scalable strategy for enhancing autonomous controller robustness in complex, data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Semantic Manipulation: Token-Space Attacks on Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02686)
*Yuheng Zhang,Mingyue Huo,Minghao Zhu,Mengxue Zhang,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOMPA是一种直接在词元空间进行对抗优化的攻击框架，通过绕过策略与奖励模型之间的解码-重新词元化接口，利用黑盒标量反馈自动发现能引发极高奖励的非语言词元模式，揭示了当前RLHF流程中的关键漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击主要在语义空间操作，构建人类可读的对抗输出以利用奖励模型偏差。本文旨在探索一种根本不同的范式：直接在词元空间进行对抗优化，以揭示奖励模型在非语义机制下的系统性漏洞。

Method: 提出Token Mapping Perturbation Attack (TOMPA)框架，绕过策略与奖励模型之间的标准解码-重新词元化接口，使攻击策略能够在原始词元序列而非连贯自然语言上进行优化。仅使用黑盒标量反馈，自动发现能引发高奖励的非语言词元模式。

Result: 针对Skywork-Reward-V2-Llama-3.1-8B模型，TOMPA几乎使GPT-5参考答案的奖励翻倍，并在98.0%的提示上超越GPT-5。尽管获得高分，生成的输出退化为无意义的文本，表明奖励模型可在语义机制之外被系统性利用。

Conclusion: TOMPA揭示了奖励模型在非语义机制下的系统性漏洞，暴露了当前RLHF流程中的关键脆弱性。这表明仅依赖奖励模型作为优化目标存在根本性缺陷，需要更鲁棒的评估机制来防止奖励黑客攻击。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are widely used as optimization targets in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), yet they remain vulnerable to reward hacking. Existing attacks mainly operate within the semantic space, constructing human-readable adversarial outputs that exploit RM biases. In this work, we introduce a fundamentally different paradigm: Token Mapping Perturbation Attack (TOMPA), a framework that performs adversarial optimization directly in token space. By bypassing the standard decode-re-tokenize interface between the policy and the reward model, TOMPA enables the attack policy to optimize over raw token sequences rather than coherent natural language. Using only black-box scalar feedback, TOMPA automatically discovers non-linguistic token patterns that elicit extremely high rewards across multiple state-of-the-art RMs. Specifically, when targeting Skywork-Reward-V2-Llama-3.1-8B, TOMPA nearly doubles the reward of GPT-5 reference answers and outperforms them on 98.0% of prompts. Despite these high scores, the generated outputs degenerate into nonsensical text, revealing that RMs can be systematically exploited beyond the semantic regime and exposing a critical vulnerability in current RLHF pipelines.

</details>


### [145] [Matrix Profile for Time-Series Anomaly Detection: A Reproducible Open-Source Benchmark on TSB-AD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02445)
*Chin-Chia Michael Yeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MMPAD是一个基于矩阵轮廓（Matrix Profile）的时间序列异常检测开源系统，针对TSB-AD基准测试设计，结合了多维聚合、kNN检索和移动平均后处理等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵轮廓方法虽然具有可解释性和可扩展性，但在时间序列异常检测中的基准性能仍依赖于超越基本最近邻轮廓的设计选择。需要开发一个可复现的参考系统，在TSB-AD基准测试上展示MP方法的有效性。

Method: 1. 预排序多维聚合；2. 高效排除区感知的k最近邻检索，用于处理重复异常；3. 移动平均后处理。系统针对单变量和多变量时间序列分别优化超参数设置。

Result: 提供了在TSB-AD基准测试上的完整实现和结果，包括单变量和多变量赛道的超参数设置。系统在总体排行榜和特定数据集特征上的表现进行了分析。

Conclusion: MMPAD为基于矩阵轮廓的时间序列异常检测提供了一个可复现的开源参考实现，在TSB-AD基准测试上展示了MP方法的有效性，并详细记录了实现细节和性能分析。

Abstract: Matrix Profile (MP) methods are an interpretable and scalable family of distance-based methods for time-series anomaly detection, but strong benchmark performance still depends on design choices beyond a vanilla nearest-neighbor profile. This technical report documents an open-source Matrix Profile for Anomaly Detection (MMPAD) submission to TSB-AD, a benchmark that covers both univariate and multivariate time series. The submitted system combines pre-sorted multidimensional aggregation, efficient exclusion-zone-aware k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) retrieval for repeated anomalies, and moving-average post-processing. To serve as a reproducible reference for MP-based anomaly detection on TSB-AD, we detail the released implementation, the hyperparameter settings for the univariate and multivariate tracks, and the corresponding benchmark results. We further analyze how the system performs on the aggregate leaderboard and across specific dataset characteristics.The open-source implementation is available at https://github.com/mcyeh/mmpad_tsb.

</details>


### [146] [SEDGE: Structural Extrapolated Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02482)
*Kun Zhang,Jiaqi Sun,Yiqing Li,Ignavier Ng,Namrata Deka,Shaoan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于数据生成过程假设的结构外推数据生成框架SEDGE，确保新规范数据可靠生成，并在保守假设下近似识别其分布


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在数据生成过程中如何可靠地生成满足新规范的数据，并识别其分布的问题，特别是在需要外推生成超出原始数据分布范围的数据时

Method: 基于结构信息优化策略或扩散后验采样的实用方法，实现外推数据生成；提供数据满足新规范可靠生成的条件和近似可识别性条件

Result: 在合成数据上验证了外推性能，并通过外推图像生成作为真实场景展示了框架的有效性

Conclusion: SEDGE框架能够在适当假设下可靠生成满足新规范的数据，并在保守假设下近似识别其分布，为外推数据生成提供了理论保证和实用方法

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for Structural Extrapolated Data GEneration (SEDGE) based on suitable assumptions on the underlying data generating process. We provide conditions under which data satisfying new specifications can be generated reliably, together with the approximate identifiability of the distribution of such data under certain ``conservative" assumptions. On the algorithmic side, we develop practical methods to achieve extrapolated data generation, based on the structure-informed optimization strategy or diffusion posterior sampling, respectively. We verify the extrapolation performance on synthetic data and also consider extrapolated image generation as a real-world scenario to illustrate the validity of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [147] [Re-analysis of the Human Transcription Factor Atlas Recovers TF-Specific Signatures from Pooled Single-Cell Screens with Missing Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02511)
*Arka Jain,Umesh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 重新分析人类TF Atlas数据集，开发可重复流程进行质量控制、MORF条形码解复用、TF差异表达和功能富集分析，使用外部基准和背景减除恢复TF特异性信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共单细胞扰动图谱是研究转录因子功能的重要资源，但下游再分析常受限于不完整的元数据和缺失的内部对照，需要开发方法从现有数据中提取可靠信息

Method: 重新分析GSE216481数据集，建立可重复流程进行质量控制、MORF条形码解复用、TF差异表达分析；使用胚胎体细胞作为外部基准，通过背景减除去除共享批次/转导伪影；采用Harmony评估批次效应

Result: 从77,018个细胞中成功分配60,997个(79.2%)到87个TF身份；恢复59/61个可测试TF的特异性信号；识别HOPX、MAZ、PAX6、FOS、FEZF2为最强转录重塑因子；发现TF与特定通路的关联；与已发表排名显著相关(Spearman ρ=-0.316, p=0.013)

Conclusion: 通过原则性外部对照、伪影去除和可重复计算，TF Atlas数据能够支持经过验证的TF特异性转录和通路分析，即使缺乏内部对照也能恢复稳健的TF水平信号

Abstract: Public pooled single-cell perturbation atlases are valuable resources for studying transcription factor (TF) function, but downstream re-analysis can be limited by incomplete deposited metadata and missing internal controls. Here we re-analyze the human TF Atlas dataset (GSE216481), a MORF-based pooled overexpression screen spanning 3,550 TF open reading frames and 254,519 cells, with a reproducible pipeline for quality control, MORF barcode demultiplexing, per-TF differential expression, and functional enrichment. From 77,018 cells in the pooled screen, we assign 60,997 (79.2\%) to 87 TF identities. Because the deposited barcode mapping lacks the GFP and mCherry negative controls present in the original library, we use embryoid body (EB) cells as an external baseline and remove shared batch/transduction artifacts by background subtraction. This strategy recovers TF-specific signatures for 59 of 61 testable TFs, compared with 27 detected by one-vs-rest alone, showing that robust TF-level signal can be rescued despite missing intra-pool controls. HOPX, MAZ, PAX6, FOS, and FEZF2 emerge as the strongest transcriptional remodelers, while per-TF enrichment links FEZF2 to regulation of differentiation, EGR1 to Hippo and cardiac programs, FOS to focal adhesion, and NFIC to collagen biosynthesis. Condition-level analyses reveal convergent Wnt, neurogenic, EMT, and Hippo signatures, and Harmony indicates minimal confounding batch effects across pooled replicates. Our per-TF effect sizes significantly agree with Joung et al.'s published rankings (Spearman $ρ= -0.316$, $p = 0.013$; negative because lower rank indicates stronger effect). Together, these results show that the deposited TF Atlas data can support validated TF-specific transcriptional and pathway analyses when paired with principled external controls, artifact removal, and reproducible computation.

</details>


### [148] [AdaHOP: Fast and Accurate Low-Precision Training via Outlier-Pattern-Aware Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02525)
*Seonggon Kim,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaHOP：基于异常值模式感知的自适应Hadamard变换方法，通过分析LLM中不同张量的异常值结构，为每个矩阵乘法分配最优的Hadamard变换方向或异常值处理策略，在MXFP4精度下实现BF16训练质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度训练方法采用固定的Hadamard变换来抑制异常值，但忽略了不同张量间异常值结构的显著差异。研究发现异常值模式在权重、激活值和梯度中呈现多样化，需要针对性的变换方向才能有效减少量化误差。

Method: 首先系统研究LLM中异常值模式，将其分为行方向、列方向和无异常值三类。基于此提出AdaHOP：为每个矩阵乘法分配最优策略——当内维度平滑有效时使用内部Hadamard变换；否则结合选择性异常值提取，将主导异常值路由到高精度路径。配合硬件感知的Triton内核实现。

Result: 在MXFP4精度下达到BF16训练质量，实现高达3.6倍的内存压缩和1.8倍的内核加速（相比BF16全精度训练）。

Conclusion: 异常值模式在LLM中具有多样性，需要自适应策略而非固定变换。AdaHOP通过模式感知的自适应Hadamard变换，在极低精度下保持训练质量，显著提升内存效率和计算速度。

Abstract: Low-precision training (LPT) commonly employs Hadamard transforms to suppress outliers and mitigate quantization error in large language models (LLMs). However, prior methods apply a fixed transform uniformly, despite substantial variation in outlier structures across tensors. Through the first systematic study of outlier patterns across weights, activations, and gradients of LLMs, we show that this strategy is fundamentally flawed: the effectiveness of Hadamard-based suppression depends on how the transform's smoothing direction aligns with the outlier structure of each operand -- a property that varies substantially across layers and computation paths. We characterize these patterns into three types: Row-wise, Column-wise, and None. Each pair requires a tailored transform direction or outlier handling strategy to minimize quantization error. Based on this insight, we propose AdaHOP (Adaptive Hadamard transform with Outlier-Pattern-aware strategy), which assigns each matrix multiplication its optimal strategy: Inner Hadamard Transform (IHT) where inner-dimension smoothing is effective, or IHT combined with selective Outlier Extraction (OE) -- routing dominant outliers to a high-precision path -- where it is not. Combined with hardware-aware Triton kernels, AdaHOP achieves BF16 training quality at MXFP4 precision while delivering up to 3.6X memory compression and 1.8X kernel acceleration} over BF16 full-precision training.

</details>


### [149] [A Spectral Framework for Multi-Scale Nonlinear Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02535)
*Zeyang Huang,Angelos Chatzimparmpas,Thomas Höllt,Takanori Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个谱框架解决降维中的全局-局部权衡和可解释性问题，通过谱基与交叉熵优化实现多尺度表示，支持谱模式分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决降维中的两个长期权衡：1) 全局-局部保持矛盾（如t-SNE/UMAP侧重局部但扭曲全局，Laplacian Eigenmaps保持全局但局部分离有限）；2) 表达能力与分析透明度之间的差距（许多非线性方法缺乏与高维结构的显式联系）

Method: 提出谱框架：结合谱基与交叉熵优化嵌入高维数据，实现多尺度表示以桥接全局和局部结构；利用线性谱分解支持通过图频率视角分析嵌入，可检查谱模式如何影响嵌入结果；补充基于字形散点图增强进行可视化探索

Result: 定量评估和案例研究表明，该框架改善了流形连续性，同时通过谱模式贡献实现了对嵌入结构的更深入分析

Conclusion: 提出的谱框架解决了降维中的全局-局部权衡和可解释性问题，通过谱分析和多尺度表示提供了更好的流形保持和嵌入过程洞察

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) is characterized by two longstanding trade-offs. First, there is a global-local preservation tension: methods such as t-SNE and UMAP prioritize local neighborhood preservation, yet may distort global manifold structure, while methods such as Laplacian Eigenmaps preserve global geometry but often yield limited local separation. Second, there is a gap between expressiveness and analytical transparency: many nonlinear DR methods produce embeddings without an explicit connection to the underlying high-dimensional structure, limiting insight into the embedding process. In this paper, we introduce a spectral framework for nonlinear DR that addresses these challenges. Our approach embeds high-dimensional data using a spectral basis combined with cross-entropy optimization, enabling multi-scale representations that bridge global and local structure.
